<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-note" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg class="octicon octicon-info mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M0 8a8 8 0 1 1 16 0A8 8 0 0 1 0 8Zm8-6.5a6.5 6.5 0 1 0 0 13 6.5 6.5 0 0 0 0-13ZM6.5 7.75A.75.75 0 0 1 7.25 7h1a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v2.75h.25a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5h.25v-2h-.25a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75ZM8 6a1 1 0 1 1 0-2 1 1 0 0 1 0 2Z"></path></svg>Note</p><p dir="auto">Termux on Android requires storage permission. Run <code>termux-setup-storage</code> if needed.</p>

sudo apt install -y nodejs git ffmpeg libvips-dev libwebp-dev build-essential

git clone https://github.com/XdKing2/star-xd.git
cd star-xd
npm install
cp sample.env .env &amp;&amp; nano .env

# Keep alive with PM2

pm2 start dist/index.js --name star-xd
pm2 save &amp;&amp; pm2 startup"><pre>curl -fsSL https://deb.nodesource.com/setup_20.x <span class="pl-k">|</span> sudo -E bash -

git clone https://github.com/XdKing2/star-xd.git
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> star-xd

pm2 save <span class="pl-k">&amp;&amp;</span> pm2 startup</pre></div>

<a href="https://quay.io/repository/mrxdking/star-xd" rel="nofollow"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/9a70a14cef449a3fce77582edfb86a0be9f64312d9e907a01dd9b073d5f3a83e/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f62616467652f517561792e696f2d4546354232353f7374796c653d666c61742d737175617265266c6f676f3d726564686174266c6f676f436f6c6f723d7768697465" alt="Quay.io" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/badge/Quay.io-EF5B25?style=flat-square&amp;logo=redhat&amp;logoColor=white" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Pull pre-built image (no build required)
docker pull quay.io/mrxdking/star-xd:latest

# Run with your credentials
docker run -d \
  -e SESSION_ID=&quot;star~YOUR_SESSION_ID&quot; \
  -e OWNER_NUMBER=&quot;YOUR_WHATSAPP_NUMBER&quot; \
  -e BOT_NAME=&quot;STAR XD&quot; \
  -e COMMAND_MODE=&quot;public&quot; \
  -p 5000:5000 \
  --name star-xd \
  quay.io/mrxdking/star-xd:latest

# View logs
docker logs -f star-xd"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Pull pre-built image (no build required)</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Run with your credentials</span>

  -e SESSION_ID=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>star~YOUR_SESSION_ID<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> \
  -e OWNER_NUMBER=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>YOUR_WHATSAPP_NUMBER<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> \

<p align="center" dir="auto">
  <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/70dbbc4c86f9fb21965740fe92e12911af0762f887880dfbad8d70bf1b0a1421/68747470733a2f2f692e696d6775722e636f6d2f644261534b57462e676966"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/70dbbc4c86f9fb21965740fe92e12911af0762f887880dfbad8d70bf1b0a1421/68747470733a2f2f692e696d6775722e636f6d2f644261534b57462e676966" height="40" width="100%" data-animated-image="" data-canonical-src="https://i.imgur.com/dBaSKWF.gif" style="max-width: 100%; height: auto; max-height: 40px;"></a>
</p>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-caution" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg class="octicon octicon-stop mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4.47.22A.749.749 0 0 1 5 0h6c.199 0 .389.079.53.22l4.25 4.25c.141.14.22.331.22.53v6a.749.749 0 0 1-.22.53l-4.25 4.25A.749.749 0 0 1 11 16H5a.749.749 0 0 1-.53-.22L.22 11.53A.749.749 0 0 1 0 11V5c0-.199.079-.389.22-.53Zm.84 1.28L1.5 5.31v5.38l3.81 3.81h5.38l3.81-3.81V5.31L10.69 1.5ZM8 4a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v3.5a.75.75 0 0 1-1.5 0v-3.5A.75.75 0 0 1 8 4Zm0 8a1 1 0 1 1 0-2 1 1 0 0 1 0 2Z"></path></svg>Caution</p><p dir="auto"><strong><g-emoji class="g-emoji" alias="warning">⚠️</g-emoji> Cloud Platform Data Loss:</strong> Heroku, Render, Railway, and similar cloud platforms reset the entire filesystem on every restart/redeploy. All local data (JSON files, session, media) will be <strong>permanently lost</strong>. Always use <strong>MongoDB</strong> or <strong>PostgreSQL</strong> for persistent data storage on cloud platforms!</p>
</div>
<details>
<summary>🗄️ STORAGE BACKENDS</summary>
<p dir="auto">Set one database URL in .env. If all are empty, JSON file storage is used automatically.</p>

  <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/70dbbc4c86f9fb21965740fe92e12911af0762f887880dfbad8d70bf1b0a1421/68747470733a2f2f692e696d6775722e636f6d2f644261534b57462e676966"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/70dbbc4c86f9fb21965740fe92e12911af0762f887880dfbad8d70bf1b0a1421/68747470733a2f2f692e696d6775722e636f6d2f644261534b57462e676966" height="40" width="100%" data-animated-image="" data-canonical-src="https://i.imgur.com/dBaSKWF.gif" style="max-width: 100%; height: auto; max-height: 40px;"></a>

<details>
<summary>🔧 TROUBLESHOOTING</summary>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Bot not connecting</strong></p>
<p dir="auto">· Verify SESSION_ID is correct and starts with star~
· Reset session: <code>npm run reset-session &amp;&amp; npm start</code>
· If using PAIRING_NUMBER, link within 60 seconds of code appearing</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Commands not responding</strong></p>
<p dir="auto">· Check you're using the right prefix (default .)
· COMMAND_MODE=private → only owner can use commands
· OWNER_NUMBER must have no + sign</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Data lost after restart</strong></p>
<p dir="auto">· Cloud platforms reset the filesystem on redeploy
· Add MONGO_URL to use MongoDB — MongoDB Atlas has a free tier</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>myAppStateKey not present error</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="node -e &quot;
const fs = require('fs');
const c = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('session/creds.json','utf8'));
delete c.myAppStateKeyId;
fs.writeFileSync('session/creds.json', JSON.stringify(c, null, 2));
console.log('Done');
&quot;
npm start"><pre>node -e <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-s">const fs = require('fs');</span>
<span class="pl-s">const c = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('session/creds.json','utf8'));</span>
<span class="pl-s">delete c.myAppStateKeyId;</span>
<span class="pl-s">fs.writeFileSync('session/creds.json', JSON.stringify(c, null, 2));</span>
<span class="pl-s">console.log('Done');</span>
<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
npm start</pre></div>
</details>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-tip" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg class="octicon octicon-light-bulb mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 1.5c-2.363 0-4 1.69-4 3.75 0 .984.424 1.625.984 2.304l.214.253c.223.264.47.556.673.848.284.411.537.896.621 1.49a.75.75 0 0 1-1.484.211c-.04-.282-.163-.547-.37-.847a8.456 8.456 0 0 0-.542-.68c-.084-.1-.173-.205-.268-.32C3.201 7.75 2.5 6.766 2.5 5.25 2.5 2.31 4.863 0 8 0s5.5 2.31 5.5 5.25c0 1.516-.701 2.5-1.328 3.259-.095.115-.184.22-.268.319-.207.245-.383.453-.541.681-.208.3-.33.565-.37.847a.751.751 0 0 1-1.485-.212c.084-.593.337-1.078.621-1.489.203-.292.45-.584.673-.848.075-.088.147-.173.213-.253.561-.679.985-1.32.985-2.304 0-2.06-1.637-3.75-4-3.75ZM5.75 12h4.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-4.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5ZM6 15.25a.75.75 0 0 1 .75-.75h2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75Z"></path></svg>Tip</p><p dir="auto">After fixing the myAppStateKey error, send any message to the bot — WhatsApp re-syncs keys automatically.</p>
